EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE CORPORATION 
The 9th January 1984 

No. U/16/53/83/Med. in(Pb.).— ^In pursuance of the Resolu¬ 
tion passed by the ESI Corporation at its meeting held on 25th 
April, 1951, conferring upon me the powers of the Corporation, 
under Regulation 105 of the ESI (General) Regulations, 1950, 
I hereby authorise the Senior Medical Officer, Civil Hospital, 
Gidderbaha (Punjab) to function as medical authority for 
Gidderbaha centre, w. e. f. the date of assumption of charge, 
till such time a Full-time Medical Referee takes over charge 
of the centre, at a monthly remuneration of Rs. 75/-, for the 
purpose of medical examination of the insured persons and grant 
of further certificates to them when the correctness of original 
certificates is in doubt. 

DR, VED PRAKASH, 
Medial Commissioner 


S.K. KAPUR, 
Joint Manager 


AIR INDIA 

AIR INDIA STAFF HOUSING REGULATIONS 1967 
No, HQ/58-5.—^In exercise of the powers coofttrred by 
Section 45(i) of the Air Corporations Act, 1953 (27 of 

1953), Air India hereby makes the following regulatiom 
further to amend the Air India Staff Housing Regulation 
I‘^67, as follows : 

(1) (i) These regulalions mf»y be called the Air-India 
Staff Housing (Amendment) Regulatioos, 
1983; 

fii) They shall come mto force from 1st Septem¬ 
ber. 1983. 

f2) In the Alr-India Staff Housing Regailatlons, 
1967— 

(i) For flic existing Regulation 9, the following 
Regulations shall be substituted, namely :— 


Part 111— Sec, 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA. .TANUARY 28. 1984 (MAGHA 8, 1905) 875 


(c) where the loan b required for enlarging the 
existing accommodation the amount of 
loan shall not exceed 75 times the monthly 
basic pay plus Variable Dearness Allow¬ 
ance at tihe time hc/she applies for the 
loan or the cost of such enlargement or 
Rs. 50,000/- whichever is less. 


0. Loan Linrits 

li) (al No individual loan shall normally exceed 
75 times the rponthly basic pay plus 
Variable Dearness Allowance of the em¬ 
ployee at the time he/she applies for the 
loan or the total cost of construction/ 
cost of ready built house/flat (inclusive 
of the cost of land) or Rs. 75,000/- 
whichever is less. 

(b) Where the individual loan applied for is 
more than Rs. 75,000/- the amount of 
loan admissible shall not exceed 60 times 
the montihly basic pay plus Variable 
Dearness Allowance of the employee at 
the time he/she applies for the loan or 
80% (Four fifths) of the total cost of 
construction/cost of ready-built house/ 
flat (inclusive of the cost of land) which¬ 
ever is less subject to a maximum of 
Rs. Z lakhs in any one case. 


tii) For the existing Regulation 21, the following 
Regulation shall be substituted, namely :— 

21. Loan Limits 

“The amount of a collective loan shall be 
determined on the basis of a max'mum of 
Rs. 2,00 lakhs per flat, proided that such 
amount shall not exceed 80 per cent of the 
total cost of the project of me Society con¬ 
cerned inclusive of the cost of land."
